<p class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">A frontier model that leads on personal and professional agents</p>
<p class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">Today, we’re excited to introduce the Muse Wonder series with Muse Wonder and Muse Wonder Full, our most capable models that deliver state-of-the-art performance on personal and professional agentic tasks. Together, they mark the next step on our scaling ladder and a major leap toward personal superintelligence. Muse Wonder brings together <strong>Active Memory</strong> and <strong>Active Focus</strong> to keep track of what matters to you and work diligently toward what you want to accomplish.</p>
<p class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">Muse Wonder is available today through the <a class="text-link underline underline-offset-4" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank" href="http://dev.meta.ai">Meta Model API</a> and <a class="text-link underline underline-offset-4" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank" href="http://dev.meta.ai/code">Muse Code</a>, and Muse Wonder Full is available today for Muse Code subscribers.</p>
<p class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">Muse Wonder will soon power experiences in <a class="text-link underline underline-offset-4" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank" href="https://muse.ai/">Muse</a>, and will roll out first to subscribers. Muse Wonder will also power artifact generation and processes that require more intelligence. And in the future, we’ll be using Muse Wonder to develop specialized models that we can bring to everyone using Muse.</p></div></div><nav aria-label="In this article" class="article-section-navigation-module__M5nXqG__navigation" data-expanded="false"><p class="article-section-navigation-module__M5nXqG__title">Contents</p><button aria-controls="_R_aav5ubrpfctb_" aria-expanded="false" class="article-section-navigation-module__M5nXqG__mobileTrigger" type="button"><span>Scaling Toward Personal Superintelligence</span><svg aria-hidden="true" fill="none" class="article-section-navigation-module__M5nXqG__link" href="#frontier-capabilities">Frontier Capabilities</a></li><li><a class="article-section-navigation-module__M5nXqG__link" Frontier</a></li></ol></nav><div class="article-section-navigation-module__M5nXqG__content"><div class="article-body-module__wNr16G__watermelonStaging space-y-6"><h2 class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__sectionHeading font-display text-text-primary pt-8 text-3xl leading-tight font-medium" data-article-section-target="true" id="scaling-toward-personal-superintelligence">Scaling Toward Personal Superintelligence</h2>
<p class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">Fourteen months ago, we began a ground-up overhaul of our artificial intelligence efforts. In that time, we’ve gone from rebuilding our stack from first principles to pushing the frontier.</p>
<figure class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__figure article-media-module__3u0F1q__wide" data-media-placement="wide"><div class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__shell" data-testid="article-media-shell"><div aria-busy="true" class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__frame" data-site-image-state="loading" data-testid="site-image-loading-frame" style="aspect-ratio:5760 / 3750"><span aria-hidden="true" class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__glimmer" data-site-image-placeholder="true" data-testid="site-image-placeholder"></span><div class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__content" data-site-image-content="true"><span aria-hidden="true" class="site-image-module__lM2DSa__image article-media-module__3u0F1q__media article-media-module__3u0F1q__contain" data-media-theme-pending="true" style="aspect-ratio:5760 / 3750;display:block;inline-size:100%"></span></div></div></div></figure>
<p class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">When we introduced <a class="text-link underline underline-offset-4" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank" href="https://ai.meta.com/blog/introducing-muse-spark-msl/">Muse Spark</a>, we outlined our approach to predictable, rigorous scaling. Today’s release builds on that work across pretraining and reinforcement learning, delivering our most capable models yet. Trained at our gigawatt-scale Prometheus data center, Muse Wonder and Muse Wonder Full bring together the work of teams across Meta to deliver frontier capabilities for complex tasks at work and in everyday life — another step toward bringing personal superintelligence to the billions of people who use our products.</p>
<h2 class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__sectionHeading font-display text-text-primary pt-8 text-3xl leading-tight font-medium" data-article-section-target="true" id="frontier-capabilities">Frontier Capabilities</h2>
<h3 class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__sectionHeading font-display text-text-primary pt-5 text-xl leading-tight font-medium" data-article-section-target="true" id="model-highlights">Model Highlights</h3>
<p class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">The Muse Wonder series achieves state-of-the-art results across key benchmarks for personal and professional agents, memory, and coding.</p>
<div class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__grid article-media-module__3u0F1q__gridStandalone article-media-module__3u0F1q__columnsThree" data-media-grid-alignment="start" data-media-grid-columns="3"><figure class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__figure article-media-module__3u0F1q__body" data-media-placement="body"><div class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__shell" <div class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__grid article-media-module__3u0F1q__gridStandalone article-media-module__3u0F1q__gridCentered article-media-module__3u0F1q__columnsThree" data-media-grid-alignment="center" data-media-grid-columns="3"><figure class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__figure article-media-module__3u0F1q__body" data-media-placement="body"><div class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__shell" data-testid="article-media-shell"><div aria-busy="true" class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__frame" data-site-image-state="loading" <p class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">The full results below compare Muse Wonder and Muse Wonder Full with other frontier models across a broad evaluation suite.</p>
<figure class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__figure article-media-module__3u0F1q__body" data-media-placement="body"><div class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__shell" data-testid="article-media-shell"><div aria-busy="true" class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__frame" data-site-image-state="loading" data-testid="site-image-loading-frame" style="aspect-ratio:3240 / 4890"><span aria-hidden="true" class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__glimmer" data-site-image-placeholder="true" data-testid="site-image-placeholder"></span><div class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__content" data-site-image-content="true"><span aria-hidden="true" class="site-image-module__lM2DSa__image article-media-module__3u0F1q__media article-media-module__3u0F1q__contain" data-media-theme-pending="true" style="aspect-ratio:3240 / 4890;display:block;inline-size:100%"></span></div></div></div></figure>
<p class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">For more details about our evaluations, see <a class="text-link underline underline-offset-4" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank" href="/static/muse-wonder-model-card">our report</a>.</p>
<p class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">The results above provide a broad view of the models’ performance. We now take a closer look at four areas central to their work on real-world tasks: domain expertise, Active Memory, Active Focus, and frontier coding.</p>
<h3 class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__sectionHeading font-display text-text-primary pt-5 text-xl leading-tight font-medium" data-article-section-target="true" id="domain-expertise">Domain Expertise</h3>
<p class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">Muse Wonder and Muse Wonder Full apply specialized knowledge to problems people encounter at work and in daily life, from understanding a lease to making sense of financial or health information. Our evaluations include tasks in law, finance, and health that test this ability. Harvey’s Legal Agent Benchmark (LAB), for example, requires models to work with source documents and follow detailed instructions to produce legal work products.</p>
<div class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__grid article-media-module__3u0F1q__gridStandalone article-media-module__3u0F1q__columnsTwo" data-media-grid-alignment="start" data-media-grid-columns="2"><figure class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__figure article-media-module__3u0F1q__body" data-media-placement="body"><div class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__shell" data-testid="article-media-shell"><div aria-busy="true" class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__frame" data-site-image-state="loading" data-testid="site-image-loading-frame" style="aspect-ratio:3240 / 3240"><span aria-hidden="true" class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__glimmer" data-site-image-placeholder="true" data-testid="site-image-placeholder"></span><div class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__content" data-site-image-content="true"><span aria-hidden="true" class="site-image-module__lM2DSa__image article-media-module__3u0F1q__media article-media-module__3u0F1q__contain" data-media-theme-pending="true" style="aspect-ratio:3240 / 3240;display:block;inline-size:100%"></span></div></div></div></figure><figure class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__figure article-media-module__3u0F1q__body" data-media-placement="body"><div class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__shell" data-testid="article-media-shell"><div aria-busy="true" class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__frame" data-site-image-state="loading" data-testid="site-image-loading-frame" style="aspect-ratio:3240 / 3240"><span aria-hidden="true" data-media-theme-pending="true" style="aspect-ratio:3240 / 3240;display:block;inline-size:100%"></span></div></div></div></figure></div>
<h3 class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__sectionHeading font-display text-text-primary pt-5 text-xl leading-tight font-medium" data-article-section-target="true" id="active-memory">Active Memory</h3>
<p class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">One of the most critical steps towards useful personal and professional agents is reliable memory. Models need to remember what matters and recognize when earlier information becomes relevant. We call this <strong>Active Memory</strong>: carrying important context about you across conversations and actively retrieving the details relevant to the task at hand.</p>
<div class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__grid article-media-module__3u0F1q__gridStandalone article-media-module__3u0F1q__gridCentered article-media-module__3u0F1q__columnsTwo" data-media-grid-alignment="center" data-media-grid-columns="2"><figure class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__figure article-media-module__3u0F1q__body" data-media-placement="body"><div class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__shell" data-testid="article-media-shell"><div aria-busy="true" class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__frame" data-site-image-state="loading" data-testid="site-image-loading-frame" style="aspect-ratio:3240 / 3240"><span aria-hidden="true" class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__glimmer" data-site-image-placeholder="true" data-testid="site-image-placeholder"></span><div class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__content" data-site-image-content="true"><span aria-hidden="true" class="site-image-module__lM2DSa__image article-media-module__3u0F1q__media article-media-module__3u0F1q__contain" data-media-theme-pending="true" style="aspect-ratio:3240 / 3240;display:block;inline-size:100%"></span></div></div></div></figure></div>
<h3 class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__sectionHeading font-display text-text-primary pt-5 text-xl leading-tight font-medium" data-article-section-target="true" id="active-focus">Active Focus</h3>
<p class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">Using that context to complete a task also requires sustained effort. <strong>Active Focus</strong> describes how Muse Wonder continues working toward a challenging goal: trying alternative approaches, working through blockers, and adapting to new information. Along the way, it asks for clarification when needed and checks in before taking important actions. We evaluate focus through FocusBench and sustained execution through SWE-Marathon, which tests work on long-running software projects.</p>
<div class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__grid article-media-module__3u0F1q__gridStandalone article-media-module__3u0F1q__columnsTwo" data-media-grid-alignment="start" data-media-grid-columns="2"><figure class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__figure article-media-module__3u0F1q__body" data-media-placement="body"><div class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__shell" data-testid="article-media-shell"><div aria-busy="true" class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__frame" data-site-image-state="loading" data-testid="site-image-loading-frame" style="aspect-ratio:3240 / 3240"><span aria-hidden="true" class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__glimmer" data-site-image-placeholder="true" data-testid="site-image-placeholder"></span><div class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__content" data-site-image-content="true"><span aria-hidden="true" class="site-image-module__lM2DSa__image article-media-module__3u0F1q__media article-media-module__3u0F1q__contain" data-media-theme-pending="true" style="aspect-ratio:3240 / 3240;display:block;inline-size:100%"></span></div></div></div></figure><figure class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__figure article-media-module__3u0F1q__body" data-media-placement="body"><div class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__shell" data-testid="article-media-shell"><div aria-busy="true" class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__frame" data-site-image-state="loading" data-testid="site-image-loading-frame" style="aspect-ratio:3240 / 3240"><span aria-hidden="true" class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__glimmer" data-site-image-placeholder="true" data-testid="site-image-placeholder"></span><div class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__content" data-site-image-content="true"><span aria-hidden="true" class="site-image-module__lM2DSa__image article-media-module__3u0F1q__media article-media-module__3u0F1q__contain" data-media-theme-pending="true" style="aspect-ratio:3240 / 3240;display:block;inline-size:100%"></span></div></div></div></figure></div>
<h3 class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__sectionHeading font-display text-text-primary pt-5 text-xl leading-tight font-medium" data-article-section-target="true" id="frontier-coding">Frontier Coding</h3>
<p class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">Coding brings these capabilities together. Building software requires tracking requirements, making changes across a codebase, and working through repeated cycles of testing and revision. Muse Wonder and Muse Wonder Full deliver frontier coding performance, supporting projects from small applications to complex software. We trained both models on diverse, long-running tasks to help them sustain progress across this process.</p>
<div class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__grid article-media-module__3u0F1q__gridStandalone article-media-module__3u0F1q__gridCentered article-media-module__3u0F1q__columnsTwo" data-media-grid-alignment="center" data-media-grid-columns="2"><figure class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__figure article-media-module__3u0F1q__body" data-media-placement="body"><div class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__shell" data-testid="article-media-shell"><div aria-busy="true" class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__frame" data-site-image-state="loading" data-testid="site-image-loading-frame" style="aspect-ratio:3240 / 3240"><span aria-hidden="true" class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__glimmer" data-site-image-placeholder="true" data-testid="site-image-placeholder"></span><div class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__content" data-site-image-content="true"><span aria-hidden="true" class="site-image-module__lM2DSa__image article-media-module__3u0F1q__media article-media-module__3u0F1q__contain" data-media-theme-pending="true" style="aspect-ratio:3240 / 3240;display:block;inline-size:100%"></span></div></div></div></figure></div>
<section aria-label="Muse demos" class="article-mixed-media-tabs-module__Qmh85a__root" data-article-section-target="true" id="game-demos"><div><div class="content-tabs-module__tJlwga__root" <h2 class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__sectionHeading font-display text-text-primary pt-8 text-3xl leading-tight font-medium" data-article-section-target="true" id="capabilities-that-generalize">Capabilities That Generalize</h2>
<p class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">Two models often have similar benchmark scores, yet one seems to handle a much wider range of unfamiliar tasks. That’s what complaints of “benchmaxxing” and praise for “big model smell” both get at: a breadth of capability that popular benchmarks in a few domains can’t fully capture.</p>
<p class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">To quantify this mismatch, we investigate how performance on tasks targeted during post-training relates to performance on unfamiliar tasks as we scale test-time compute. The plot below compares Muse Spark 1.3 and Muse Wonder Full, with each point denoting performance at a distinct reasoning effort. The horizontal axis shows the model’s score on a composite representative of the tasks we focused on during post-training (“in-distribution”). The vertical axis shows the average score on a set of tasks we did not target during post-training (“out-of-distribution”).</p>
<p class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">Both of our models improve similarly on out-of-distribution tasks as we scale test-time compute. But given its in-distribution performance, Muse Wonder Full performs even better on unfamiliar tasks than the Muse Spark 1.3 trend would predict. This upward shift is because Muse Wonder and Muse Wonder Full build on larger, stronger base models: a stronger base yields post-training gains that carry over more effectively to unfamiliar tasks. That versatility especially matters for personal agents, which need to handle many combinations of goals, tools, and constraints — not all of which we can anticipate during training.</p>
<figure class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__figure article-media-module__3u0F1q__wide" data-media-placement="wide"><div class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__shell" data-testid="article-media-shell"><div aria-busy="true" class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__frame" data-site-image-state="loading" data-testid="site-image-loading-frame" style="aspect-ratio:5760 / 3240"><span aria-hidden="true" class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__glimmer" data-site-image-placeholder="true" data-testid="site-image-placeholder"></span><div class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__content" data-site-image-content="true"><span aria-hidden="true" class="site-image-module__lM2DSa__image article-media-module__3u0F1q__media article-media-module__3u0F1q__contain" data-media-theme-pending="true" style="aspect-ratio:5760 / 3240;display:block;inline-size:100%"></span></div></div></div></figure>

<h2 class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__sectionHeading font-display text-text-primary pt-8 text-3xl leading-tight font-medium" data-article-section-target="true" id="safety-at-the-frontier">Safety at the Frontier</h2>
<p class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">Delivering personal superintelligence to everyone requires a significant investment in alignment and safety. A personal agent takes actions on a user’s behalf and might handle context such as messages, calendars, and private documents. That level of access demands a model that exercises discretion with private information and resists misuse. We trained the Muse Wonder family to lead on both dimensions, with robustness to adversarial instructions and careful handling of private information.</p>
<div class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__grid article-media-module__3u0F1q__gridStandalone article-media-module__3u0F1q__columnsTwo" data-media-grid-alignment="start" data-media-grid-columns="2"><figure class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__figure article-media-module__3u0F1q__body" data-media-placement="body"><div class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__shell" data-testid="article-media-shell"><div aria-busy="true" class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__frame" data-site-image-state="loading" data-testid="site-image-loading-frame" style="aspect-ratio:3240 / 3240"><span aria-hidden="true" class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__glimmer" data-site-image-placeholder="true" data-testid="site-image-placeholder"></span><div class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__content" data-site-image-content="true"><span aria-hidden="true" class="site-image-module__lM2DSa__image article-media-module__3u0F1q__media article-media-module__3u0F1q__contain" data-media-theme-pending="true" style="aspect-ratio:3240 / 3240;display:block;inline-size:100%"></span></div></div></div></figure><figure class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__figure article-media-module__3u0F1q__body" data-media-placement="body"><div class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__shell" data-testid="article-media-shell"><div aria-busy="true" class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__frame" data-site-image-state="loading" data-testid="site-image-loading-frame" style="aspect-ratio:3240 / 3240"><span aria-hidden="true" class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__glimmer" data-site-image-placeholder="true" data-testid="site-image-placeholder"></span><div class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__content" data-site-image-content="true"><span aria-hidden="true" class="site-image-module__lM2DSa__image article-media-module__3u0F1q__media article-media-module__3u0F1q__contain" data-media-theme-pending="true" style="aspect-ratio:3240 / 3240;display:block;inline-size:100%"></span></div></div></div></figure></div>
<p class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">As models become more capable, our goal is to help people harness those capabilities safely and responsibly. Muse Wonder offers our strongest safeguards to date against CBRN (chemical, biological, radiological, and nuclear) and cyber misuse, while preserving access to frontier cyber capabilities for defensive use, per our <a class="text-link underline underline-offset-4" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank" href="https://research.meta.ai/static/meta-superintelligence-scaling-framework">Meta Superintelligence Scaling Framework</a>.</p>
<div class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__grid article-media-module__3u0F1q__gridStandalone article-media-module__3u0F1q__columnsThree" data-media-grid-alignment="start" data-media-grid-columns="3"><figure class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__figure article-media-module__3u0F1q__body" data-media-placement="body"><div class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__shell" data-testid="article-media-shell"><div aria-busy="true" class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__frame" data-site-image-state="loading" data-testid="site-image-loading-frame" style="aspect-ratio:3240 / 3240"><span aria-hidden="true" class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__glimmer" data-site-image-placeholder="true" data-testid="site-image-placeholder"></span><div class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__content" data-site-image-content="true"><span aria-hidden="true" class="site-image-module__lM2DSa__image article-media-module__3u0F1q__media article-media-module__3u0F1q__contain" data-media-theme-pending="true" style="aspect-ratio:3240 / 3240;display:block;inline-size:100%"></span></div></div></div></figure><figure class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__figure article-media-module__3u0F1q__body" data-media-placement="body"><div class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__shell" data-testid="article-media-shell"><div aria-busy="true" class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__frame" data-site-image-state="loading" data-testid="site-image-loading-frame" style="aspect-ratio:3240 / 3240"><span aria-hidden="true" class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__glimmer" data-site-image-placeholder="true" data-testid="site-image-placeholder"></span><div class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__content" data-site-image-content="true"><span aria-hidden="true" class="site-image-module__lM2DSa__image article-media-module__3u0F1q__media article-media-module__3u0F1q__contain" data-media-theme-pending="true" style="aspect-ratio:3240 / 3240;display:block;inline-size:100%"></span></div></div></div></figure><figure class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__figure article-media-module__3u0F1q__body" data-media-placement="body"><div class="article-media-module__3u0F1q__shell" data-testid="article-media-shell"><div aria-busy="true" class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__frame" data-site-image-state="loading" data-testid="site-image-loading-frame" style="aspect-ratio:3240 / 3240"><span aria-hidden="true" class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__glimmer" data-site-image-placeholder="true" data-testid="site-image-placeholder"></span><div class="site-image-loading-frame-module__N43ksW__content" data-site-image-content="true"><span aria-hidden="true" class="site-image-module__lM2DSa__image article-media-module__3u0F1q__media article-media-module__3u0F1q__contain" data-media-theme-pending="true" style="aspect-ratio:3240 / 3240;display:block;inline-size:100%"></span></div></div></div></figure></div>
<p class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">Muse Wonder is our most aligned model to date, and alignment is central to how we build personal superintelligence. A full report with additional safety evaluations will be available soon.</p>
<p class="article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">We hope that Muse Wonder and Muse Wonder Full give more people the power to pursue their dreams and build the world they want. This is a major technical step toward personal superintelligence for everyone, with much more to come.</p>

That’s what complaints of “benchmaxxing” and praise for “big model smell” both get at: a breadth of capability that popular benchmarks in a few domains can’t fully capture.\"}]\n63:[\"$\",\"p\",null,{\"className\":\"article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8\",\"children\":\"To quantify this mismatch, we investigate how performance on tasks targeted during post-training relates to performance on unfamiliar tasks as we scale test-time compute. The plot below compares Muse Spark 1.3 and Muse Wonder Full, with each point denoting performance at a distinct reasoning effort. The horizontal axis shows the model’s score on a composite representative of the tasks we focused on during post-training (“in-distribution”). The vertical axis shows the average score on a set of tasks we did not target during post-training (“out-of-distribution”).\"}]\n64:[\"$\",\"p\",null,{\"className\":\"article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8\",\"children\":\"Both of our models improve similarly on out-of-distribution tasks as we scale test-time compute. But given its in-distribution performance, Muse Wonder Full performs even better on unfamiliar tasks than the Muse Spark 1.3 trend would predict. This upward shift is because Muse Wonder and Muse Wonder Full build on larger, stronger base models: a stronger base yields post-training gains that carry over more effectively to unfamiliar tasks. That versatility especially matters for personal agents, which need to handle many combinations of goals, tools, and constraints — not all of which we can anticipate during training.\"}]\n67:[\"$\",\"h2\",null,{\"className\":\"article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__sectionHeading font-display text-text-primary pt-8 text-3xl leading-tight font-medium\",\"data-article-section-target\":true,\"id\":\"safety-at-the-frontier\",\"children\":\"Safety at the Frontier\"}]\n68:[\"$\",\"p\",null,{\"className\":\"article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8\",\"children\":\"Delivering personal superintelligence to everyone requires a significant investment in alignment and safety. A personal agent takes actions on a user’s behalf and might handle context such as messages, calendars, and private documents. That level of access demands a model that exercises discretion with private information and resists misuse. We trained the Muse Wonder family to lead on both dimensions, with robustness to adversarial instructions and careful handling of private information.\"}]\n6a:[\"$\",\"p\",null,{\"className\":\"article-mdx-components-module__WVV0eW__proseParagraph text-text-paragraph text-base leading-7 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8\",\"children\":[\"As models become more capable, our goal is to help people harness those capabilities safely and responsibly.
